#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));

                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+            }
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }
 
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (!std::isfinite(actual) || relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            const char* value = argv[++i];
            char* end = nullptr;
            errno = 0;
            const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(value, &end, 10);
            if (errno || end == value || *end != '\0' || *value == '-' || parsed == 0 ||
                parsed > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
                fprintf(stderr, "Matrix size must be a positive integer.\n");
                return 1;
            }
            N = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N / sizeof(double)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Matrix size is too large.\n");
        return 1;
    }

    try {
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    // Allocate and upload outside the computation timer, just like host setup.
    CudaMatmul gpu(A, B, C, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    gpu.matrixMultiply();
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    const double seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / seconds / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }

    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Error: %s\n", error.what());
        return 1;
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+namespace {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+            }
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
         }
     }
 }
 
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};

+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

-            double sum = 0.0;

-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};

+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

            const char* value = argv[++i];
            char* end = nullptr;
            errno = 0;
            const unsigned long long parsed = strtoull(value, &end, 10);
            if (errno || end == value || *end != '\0' || *value == '-' || parsed == 0 ||
                parsed > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
                fprintf(stderr, "Matrix size must be a positive integer.\n");
                return 1;
            }
            N = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;

            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }

    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");

        gpu.matrixMultiply();

        auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
        auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);

        printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());

        // Calculate GFLOPS

                return 0;

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
