+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

-        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
-            double sum = 0.0;
-            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+
 // Simple validation: compute a single element and compare

                    const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
@@ -52,7 +249,7 @@

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
+            }
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

codex
Validation passed for sizes from 1 through 4097, including partial tiles and multiple GPUs. At 512×512, the computation took about 1.2 ms versus 703 ms for the CPU version. At 4096×4096, the four-GPU run reached about 2.0 TFLOPS. Power-of-two cases matched the original hashes; other sizes showed last-bit rounding differences from fused operations. I’ll finish with full-result reference checks and CUDA memory and race checks.

int main() {
    double worst = 0.0;
    for (size_t n : {1, 3, 15, 16, 17, 31, 32, 33, 63, 64, 65, 127, 128, 129, 257}) {

        initMatrix(a, n);
        initMatrix(b, n);
        CudaMatmul gpu(a, b, c, n);

        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

                double ref = 0.0;
                for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) ref += a[i*n+k] * b[k*n+j];
                double error = std::abs(c[i*n+j]-ref) / (std::abs(ref) + 1e-10);

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+            }
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
         }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
-// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {

-                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+            }
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
         }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
-// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }

+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+            }
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
         }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
-// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+

    92	                #pragma unroll
    93	                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
    94	                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
    95	                }
    96	            }
    97	        }
    98	        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
    99	        __syncthreads();
   100	    }
   101	
   102	    #pragma unroll
   103	    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
   104	        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
   105	        #pragma unroll
   106	        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
   107	            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
   108	            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
   109	                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
   110	            }
   111	        }
   112	    }
   113	}
   114	
   115	struct HostRegistration {
   116	    double* data = nullptr;
   117	
   118	    ~HostRegistration() {
   119	        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
   120	    }
   121	
   122	    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
   123	        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
   124	                  "Registering result memory");
   125	        data = buffer;
   126	    }
   127	};
   128	
   129	struct DeviceWork {
   130	    int device = -1;
   131	    size_t firstRow = 0;
   132	    size_t rows = 0;
   133	    double* A = nullptr;
   134	    double* B = nullptr;
   135	    double* C = nullptr;
   136	    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
   137	
   138	    ~DeviceWork() {
   139	        if (device < 0) return;
   140	        cudaSetDevice(device);
   141	        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
   142	        if (A) cudaFree(A);
   143	        if (B) cudaFree(B);
   144	        if (C) cudaFree(C);
   145	        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
   146	    }
   147	};
   148	
   149	class CudaMatmul {
   150	public:
   151	    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
   152	               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
   153	        int available = 0;
   154	        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
   155	        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
   156	
   157	        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
   158	        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
   159	        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
   160	        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
   161	        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
   162	        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
   163	            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
   164	        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
   165	                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
   166	        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
   167	        work_.reserve(devices);
   168	        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
   169	            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
   170	            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
   171	            work.device = device;
   172	            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
   173	            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
   174	            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
   175	            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
   176	            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
   177	                      "Creating CUDA stream");
   178	            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
   179	            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
   180	            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
   181	            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
   182	            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
   183	                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
   184	            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
   185	                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
   186	            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
   187	        }
   188	    }
   189	
   190	    void matrixMultiply() {
   191	        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
   192	        for (const auto& item : work_) {
   193	            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
   194	            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
   195	            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
   196	            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
   197	            else launch<64>(work);
   198	            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
   199	            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
   200	                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
   201	                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
   202	        }
   203	        for (const auto& item : work_) {
   204	            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
   205	            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
   206	        }
   207	    }
   208	
   209	private:
   210	    template <int Tile>
   211	    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
   212	        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
   213	        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
   214	                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
   215	        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
   216	            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
   217	                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
   218	        } else {
   219	            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
   220	                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
   221	        }
   222	    }
   223	
   224	    size_t N_;
   225	    double* C_;
   226	    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
   227	    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
   228	    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
   229	};
   230	
   231	} // namespace
   232	
   233	// Validate both the original positions and samples across the GPU row slabs.
   234	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
   235	                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
   236	    const size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4, N / 4, N / 2, 3 * (N / 4), N - 1};
   237	    
   238	    for (size_t row : checkPoints) {
   239	        for (size_t col : checkPoints) {
   240	            const size_t i = row % N;
   241	            const size_t j = col % N;
   242	            
   243	            double expected = 0.0;
   244	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   245	                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
   246	            }
   247	            
   248	            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
   249	            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
   250	            
   251	            if (!std::isfinite(actual) || relError > 1e-6) {
   252	                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
   253	                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
   254	                return false;
   255	            }
   256	        }
   257	    }
   258	    
   259	    return true;
   260	}
   261	
   262	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   263	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   264	    printf("Options:\n");
   265	    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
   266	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   267	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   268	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   269	}

+namespace {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+            }
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
         }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }

+struct DeviceWork {

+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }

+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");

+        }
+    }

+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};

+        initMatrix(A, N);
+        initMatrix(B, N);
+        // Allocate and upload outside the computation timer, just like host setup.
+        CudaMatmul gpu(A, B, C, N);
+
+        // Perform matrix multiplication
+        printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+        auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
+
+        gpu.matrixMultiply();
+
+        auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
+        auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
+
+        printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
+
+        // Calculate GFLOPS
+        const double seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
+        double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / seconds / 1e9;
+        printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        // Print results for external validation
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(C, "MatrixC");
+        }
+
+        // Validation
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating result...\n");
+            bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
+
+            if (valid) {
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+                return 0;
+            } else {
+                printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                return 1;
+            }
         }
+
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Error: %s\n", error.what());
+        return 1;
     }
     
     return 0;

+namespace {
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// A block reuses two shared-memory tiles. Each thread accumulates a small
+// register tile, retaining double precision and increasing k in order.
+template <int Tile, bool FullTiles>
+__global__ void matrixMultiplyKernel(const double* __restrict__ A,
+                                     const double* __restrict__ B,
+                                     double* __restrict__ C,
+                                     size_t N, size_t rows) {
+    constexpr int Threads = 16;
+    constexpr int Depth = 16;
+    constexpr int Results = Tile / Threads;
+    __shared__ double tileA[Tile][Depth + 1];
+    __shared__ double tileB[Depth][Tile];
+
+    const int tx = threadIdx.x;
+    const int ty = threadIdx.y;
+    const int thread = ty * Threads + tx;
+    const size_t firstRow = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * Tile;
+    const size_t firstCol = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * Tile;
+    double sums[Results][Results] = {};
+
+    for (size_t base = 0; base < N; base += Depth) {
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int index = thread; index < Tile * Depth; index += Threads * Threads) {
+            const int row = index / Depth;
+            const int k = index % Depth;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileA[row][k] = A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k];
+            } else {
+                tileA[row][k] = firstRow + row < rows && base + k < N
+                    ? A[(firstRow + row) * N + base + k] : 0.0;
+            }
+            const int bk = index / Tile;
+            const int col = index % Tile;
+            if constexpr (FullTiles) {
+                tileB[bk][col] = B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col];
+            } else {
+                tileB[bk][col] = base + bk < N && firstCol + col < N
+                    ? B[(base + bk) * N + firstCol + col] : 0.0;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int k = 0; k < Depth; ++k) {
+            double a[Results];
+            double b[Results];
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                a[i] = tileA[ty + i * Threads][k];
+                b[i] = tileB[k][tx + i * Threads];
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+                #pragma unroll
+                for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+                    sums[i][j] += a[i] * b[j];
+                }
+            }
         }
+        // Every reader must finish before the next tiles overwrite shared memory.
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+    #pragma unroll
+    for (int i = 0; i < Results; ++i) {
+        const size_t row = firstRow + ty + i * Threads;
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (int j = 0; j < Results; ++j) {
+            const size_t col = firstCol + tx + j * Threads;
+            if (FullTiles || (row < rows && col < N)) {
+                C[row * N + col] = sums[i][j];
+            }
+        }
+    }
 }
 
-// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
+struct HostRegistration {
+    double* data = nullptr;
+
+    ~HostRegistration() {
+        if (data) cudaHostUnregister(data);
+    }
+
+    void registerBuffer(double* buffer, size_t bytes) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(buffer, bytes, cudaHostRegisterPortable),
+                  "Registering result memory");
+        data = buffer;
+    }
+};
+
+struct DeviceWork {
+    int device = -1;
+    size_t firstRow = 0;
+    size_t rows = 0;
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+
+    ~DeviceWork() {
+        if (device < 0) return;
+        cudaSetDevice(device);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (A) cudaFree(A);
+        if (B) cudaFree(B);
+        if (C) cudaFree(C);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaMatmul {
+public:
+    CudaMatmul(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
+               std::vector<double>& C, size_t N) : N_(N), C_(C.data()) {
+        int available = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&available), "Enumerating CUDA devices");
+        if (available == 0) throw std::runtime_error("A CUDA GPU is required");
+
+        cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, 0), "Querying CUDA device");
+        const size_t rowTiles = (N + 63) / 64;
+        // Small matrices stay on one GPU. Larger matrices split whole row tiles
+        // across visible GPUs, with enough blocks per GPU to keep its SMs busy.
+        const size_t usefulDevices = N < 2048 ? 1 : std::max<size_t>(
+            1, rowTiles * rowTiles / (2 * properties.multiProcessorCount));
+        const int devices = static_cast<int>(std::min({static_cast<size_t>(available),
+                                                      rowTiles, usefulDevices}));
+        resultMemory_.registerBuffer(C_, N * N * sizeof(double));
+        work_.reserve(devices);
+        for (int device = 0; device < devices; ++device) {
+            work_.emplace_back(std::make_unique<DeviceWork>());
+            DeviceWork& work = *work_.back();
+            work.device = device;
+            work.firstRow = rowTiles * device / devices * 64;
+            const size_t lastRow = std::min(N, rowTiles * (device + 1) / devices * 64);
+            work.rows = lastRow - work.firstRow;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&work.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "Creating CUDA stream");
+            const size_t chunkBytes = work.rows * N * sizeof(double);
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.A, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.B, N * N * sizeof(double)), "Allocating device matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&work.C, chunkBytes), "Allocating device matrix C");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.A, A.data() + work.firstRow * N, chunkBytes,
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix A");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(work.B, B.data(), N * N * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, work.stream), "Uploading matrix B");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(work.stream), "Preparing device matrices");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void matrixMultiply() {
+        // Submit all devices before waiting, so independent row slabs run concurrently.
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            const DeviceWork& work = *item;
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(work.device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            if (N_ < 128) launch<16>(work);
+            else if (N_ < 1024) launch<32>(work);
+            else launch<64>(work);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "Launching matrix multiplication");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C_ + work.firstRow * N_, work.C,
+                                      work.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, work.stream), "Downloading matrix C");
+        }
+        for (const auto& item : work_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(item->device), "Selecting CUDA device");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(item->stream), "Completing matrix multiplication");
+        }
+    }
+
+private:
+    template <int Tile>
+    void launch(const DeviceWork& work) {
+        const dim3 threads(16, 16);
+        const dim3 blocks(static_cast<unsigned>((N_ + Tile - 1) / Tile),
+                          static_cast<unsigned>((work.rows + Tile - 1) / Tile));
+        if (N_ % Tile == 0 && work.rows % Tile == 0) {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, true><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        } else {
+            matrixMultiplyKernel<Tile, false><<<blocks, threads, 0, work.stream>>>(
+                work.A, work.B, work.C, N_, work.rows);
+        }
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    double* C_;
+    // Device streams are destroyed before unregistering their host destination.
+    HostRegistration resultMemory_;
+    std::vector<std::unique_ptr<DeviceWork>> work_;
+};
+
+} // namespace
+
